## assistant

I mean **questions missing from the selection dropdown**, not necessarily missing from the passage itself.

Your screenshot shows **Question 1, Question 2, then Question 4**. Question 3 is skipped.

During the comparison, I checked **PrepTest 140, Section 4**:

- LSAT Lab’s dropdown lists all **27 questions**.
- LSAT U’s dropdown lists **25**, skipping **Questions 3 and 17**.
- Both questions exist in LSAT U’s underlying passage files.

The cause is an outdated generated lookup list. The dropdown reads that list, which wasn’t updated to include those questions.

So “restore missing questions” means **update the selector’s list so existing questions can be found and opened**. It does not mean inventing questions or changing answer keys.

[]) {\\n+  for (const passage of test.passages ?? []) {\\n+    const id = Number(passage.passageId);\\n+    if (metadataByPassage.has(id)) {\\n+      recordError(\'rc-index.json\', `duplicate passage ${id}`);\\n+      continue;\\n+    }\\n+    metadataByPassage.set(id, {\\n+      pt: Number(test.preptest),\\n+      section: Number(passage.section),\\n+      setNumber: Number(passage.setNumber),\\n+      questionIds: passage.questionIds ?? []) {\\n+    const passageId = Number(passage.passageId);\\n+    const meta = metadataByPassage.get(passageId);\\n+    if (!meta) {\\n+      recordError(file, `passage ${passageId} is missing from rc-index.json`);\\n+      continue;\\n+    }\\n+    const ptFromFile = Number(file.match(/\\\\d+/)[0]);\\n+    if (ptFromFile !== meta.pt) recordError(file, `passage ${passageId} belongs to PT${meta.pt}, not PT${ptFromFile}`);\\n+    for (const question of passage.questions ?? []) {\\n+      const id = Number(question.questionId);\\n+      const row = {\\n+        ...meta,\\n+        passageId,\\n+        position: Number(question.position),\\n+        questionType: question.questionType ?? null,\\n+        questionTypeFamily: question.questionTypeFamily ?? null,\\n+        difficulty: question.difficulty ?? null,\\n+        features: (question.features ?? []).map((question) => Number(question.questionId)));\\n+\\n+const searchIds = new Set();\\n+const searchAddresses = new Map();\\n+for (const question of searchIndex.questions ?? []) {\\n+  const id = Number(question.questionId);\\n+  const row = {\\n+    pt: Number(question.preptest), section: Number(question.section),\\n+    setNumber: Number(question.setNumber), position: Number(question.position),\\n+  };\\n+  const key = address(row);\\n+  if (searchIds.has(id)) recordError(\'rc-question-index.json\', `duplicate questionId ${id} at ${key}`);\\n+  searchIds.add(id);\\n+  if (searchAddresses.has(key)) recordError(\'rc-question-index.json\', `duplicate address ${key}`);\\n+  searchAddresses.set(key, id);\\n+  const expected = expectedById.get(id);\\n+  if (expected && (address(expected) !== key || expected.passageId !== Number(question.passageId))) {\\n+    recordError(\'rc-question-index.json\', `question ${id} address mismatch; expected ${address(expected)}, found ${key}`);\\n+  }\\n+  if (expected) {\\n+    const fields = [\'questionType\', \'questionTypeFamily\', \'difficulty\', \'prompt\'];\\n+    for (const field of fields) {\\n+      if ((question[field] ?? null) !== expected[field]) {\\n+        recordError(\'rc-question-index.json\', `question ${id} has stale ${field} at ${key}`);\\n+      }\\n+    }\\n+    if (JSON.stringify(question.features ?? []) !== JSON.stringify(expected.features)) {\\n+      recordError(\'rc-question-index.json\', `question ${id} has stale features at ${key}`);\\n+    }\\n+  }\\n+}\\n+\\n+const outputAddressIds = new Map();\\n+const outputPassages = new Set();\\n+for (const test of addressIndex.tests ?? []) {\\n+  for (const section of test.sections ?? []) {\\n+    for (const passage of section.rc ?? []) {\\n+      const passageKey = `PT${Number(test.pt)}S${Number(section.section)}P${Number(passage.position)}`;\\n+      const expectedPassage = expectedPassages.get(passageKey);\\n+      outputPassages.add(passageKey);\\n+      if (!expectedPassage) recordError(\'finder-address-index.json\', `unexpected passage ${passageKey}`);\\n+      else if (expectedPassage.id !== Number(passage.id) || expectedPassage.title !== (passage.title ?? null)) {\\n+        recordError(\'finder-address-index.json\', `stale passage metadata at ${passageKey}`);\\n+      }\\n+      for (const question of passage.questions ?? []) {\\n+        const row = {\\n+          pt: Number(test.pt), section: Number(section.section),\\n+          setNumber: Number(passage.position), position: Number(question.number),\\n+        };\\n+        const key = address(row);\\n+        const id = Number(question.id);\\n+        if (outputAddressIds.has(id)) recordError(\'finder-address-index.json\', `duplicate questionId ${id} at ${key}`);\\n+        else outputAddressIds.set(id, key);\\n+        if (expectedById.has(id) && address(expectedById.get(id)) !== key) {\\n+          recordError(\'finder-address-index.json\', `question ${id} address mismatch; expected ${address(expectedById.get(id))}, found ${key}`);\\n+        }\\n+      }\\n+    }\\n+  }\\n+}\\n+compareIdSets(\'finder-address-index.json\', outputAddressIds.keys());\\n+const missingPassages = [...expectedPassages.keys()].filter((key) => !outputPassages.has(key));\\n+if (missingPassages.length) recordError(\'finder-address-index.json\', `missing passage addresses: ${missingPassages.slice(0, 12).join(\', \')}`);\\n+\\n+const bankIds = new Set(bankQuestionIds.keys());\\n+const indexedBankIds = new Set(indexQuestionIds.keys());\\n+const bankMissing = [...bankIds].filter((id) => !indexedBankIds.has(id));\\n+const indexExtra = [...indexedBankIds].filter((id) => !bankIds.has(id));\\n+if (bankMissing.length || indexExtra.length) {\\n+  const format = (id) => `${id} (${address(bankQuestionIds.get(id) ?? indexQuestionIds.get(id))})`;\\n+  recordError(\'RC bank vs rc-index.json\', `mismatch; missing ${bankMissing.length}: ${bankMissing.slice(0, 12).map(format).join(\', \') || \'none\'}; extra ${indexExtra.length}: ${indexExtra.slice(0, 12).map(format).join(\', \') || \'none\'}`);\\n+}\\n+\\n+if (failures.length) {\\n+  console.error(`RC index verification failed (${failures.length} issue${failures.length === 1 ? \'\' : \'s\'}):`);\\n+  for (const failure of failures) console.error(`- ${failure}`);\\n+  process.exit(1);\\n+}\\n+\\n+console.log(\\n+  `RC indexes verified: ${bankQuestionIds.size} unique bank questions, ` +\\n+  `${searchIds.size} search records, ${outputAddressIds.size} finder addresses; ` +\\n+  `ID and official-address sets match exactly.`,\\n+);\\ndiff --git a/scripts/verify-rc-search-index.test.mjs b/scripts/verify-rc-search-index.test.mjs\\nnew file mode 100644\\nindex 00000000..871274db\\n--- /dev/null\\n+++ b/scripts/verify-rc-search-index.test.mjs\\n@@ -0,0 +1,88 @@\\n+import assert from \'node:assert/strict\';\\n+import { spawnSync } from \'node:child_process\';\\n+import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from \'node:fs\';\\n+import { tmpdir } from \'node:os\';\\n+import { join } from \'node:path\';\\n+import { fileURLToPath } from \'node:url\';\\n+import test from \'node:test\';\\n+\\n+const repoRoot = join(fileURLToPath(new URL(\'.\', import.meta.url)), \'..\');\\n+const verifier = join(repoRoot, \'scripts\', \'verify-rc-search-index.mjs\');\\n+\\n+function fixture() {\\n+  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), \'rc-index-check-\'));\\n+  const rcDir = join(root, \'public\', \'data\', \'rc\');\\n+  mkdirSync(rcDir, { recursive: true });\\n+  writeFileSync(join(rcDir, \'rc-index.json\'), JSON.stringify({\\n+    preptests: [{ preptest: 1, passages: [{ passageId: 10, section: 2, rc: [{ id: 10, position: 1, title: \'Passage\', questions: [{ id: 100, number: 1 }] }] }] }],\\n+  }));\\n+  return root;\\n+}\\n+\\n+function run(root) {\\n+  return spawnSync(process.execPath, [verifier], {\\n+    encoding: \'utf8\',\\n+    env: { ...process.env, RC_INDEX_ROOT: root },\\n+  });\\n+}\\n+\\n+test(\'accepts complete, unique bank/search/address Leave it intact and outside each ticket\'s commit.\\r\\n\\r\\nPreserve the existing18px /1.6 reader default, responsive passage-only layout, saved text-size preferences, readable passage rows, Preview drill → Start this drill sequence, suppressed homework inside active setup, direct Question details, and actor-filtered personal history. Rework only the behavior each ticket owns.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Decisions the executor can rely on\\r\\n\\r\\n- A question lookup is a read-only task. Changing a selector retains its value and creates no session, answer, annotation or attempt. Opening a preview reveals no correct answer or authored summary.\\r\\n- Starting a saved drill is deliberate and separate. Show its complete passage/question count before starting. The chosen official question can be the initial focus; make clear the whole passage is included.\\r\\n- Use the existing shared finder as the single lookup surface. The landing page provides a compact Open a question entry and a separate Create RC drill entry. Both sidebar and landing lookup entries open the same finder.\\r\\n- Group RC questions by passage, with title and official range. Auto-select a unique RC section. Clear dependent selections when bank/test changes. Keep selected addresses visible.\\r\\n- Validate return destinations as internal routes and preserve the originating page. A read-only route must remain read-only on refresh and direct entry.\\r\\n- Maintain official question identity separately from drill ordinal. [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 14.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"a7fc82","wall_time_seconds":0.3739486,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":888,"output":"#!/usr/bin/env node\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Derives public/data/rc/rc-question-index.json from the already-built RC bank\\r\\n * (rc-index.json + RC-PT<n>.json). The search page needs per-question metadata\\r\\n * (type, family, features, prompt) without loading all 59 PrepTest files; this\\r\\n * emits one flat record per drillable question.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Only questions present in the bank are indexed — those all carry a written\\r\\n * explanation and answer choices, so every search result can be drilled.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Usage: node scripts/build-rc-search-index.mjs\\r\\n */\\r\\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync, readdirSync } from \'node:fs\';\\r\\nimport { dirname, join } from \'node:path\';\\r\\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \'node:url\';\\r\\n\\r\\n// URL.pathname is percent-encoded and begins with `/C:/` on Windows. Convert\\r\\n// the module URL to a native filesystem path before joining input/output paths.\\r\\nconst RC_DIR = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), \'..\', \'public\', \'data\', \'rc\');\\r\\n\\r\\nconst index = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(RC_DIR, \'rc-index.json\'), \'utf8\'));\\r\\n\\r\\n// passageId -> index row, for PT/section/set stamping and cross-checking.\\r\\nconst passageMeta = new Map();\\r\\nfor (const pt of index.preptests) {\\r\\n  for (const p of pt.passages) {\\r\\n    passageMeta.set(p.passageId, {\\r\\n      preptest: pt.preptest,\\r\\n      section: p.section,\\r\\n      setNumber: p.setNumber,\\r\\n      questionIds: p.questionIds,\\r\\n    });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst entries = [];\\r\\nconst seen = new Set();\\r\\n\\r\\nconst files = readdirSync(RC_DIR)\\r\\n  .filter((f) => /^RC-PT\\\\d+\\\\.json$/.test(f))\\r\\n  .sort((a, b) => Number(a.match(/\\\\d+/)[0]) - Number(b.match(/\\\\d+/)[0]));\\r\\n\\r\\nfor (const file of files) {\\r\\n  const data = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(RC_DIR, file), \'utf8\'));\\r\\n  for (const passage of data.passages) {\\r\\n    const meta = passageMeta.get(passage.passageId);\\r\\n    if (!meta) throw new Error(`${file}: passage ${passage.passageId} missing from rc-index.json`);\\r\\n    for (const q of passage.questions) {\\r\\n      if (seen.has(q.questionId)) throw new Error(`${file}: duplicate questionId ${q.questionId}`);\\r\\n      seen.add(q.questionId);\\r\\n      entries.push({\\r\\n        questionId: q.questionId,\\r\\n        passageId: passage.passageId,\\r\\n        preptest: meta.preptest,\\r\\n        section: meta.section,\\r\\n        setNumber: meta.setNumber,\\r\\n        position: q.position,\\r\\n        questionType: q.questionType,\\r\\n        questionTypeFamily: q.questionTypeFamily,\\r\\n        difficulty: q.difficulty ?? null,\\r\\n        features: (q.features ?? []).map((f) => f.name),\\r\\n        prompt: q.prompt ?? null,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// Cross-check: the index\'s questionIds and the emitted set must agree exactly,\\r\\n// so the hub and the search page can never disagree about what is drillable.\\r\\nconst indexIds = new Set();\\r\\nfor (const { questionIds } of passageMeta.values()) {\\r\\n  for (const id of questionIds) {\\r\\n    if (indexIds.has(id)) throw new Error(`rc-index.json: duplicate questionId ${id}`);\\r\\n    indexIds.add(id);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\nconst missing = [...indexIds].filter((id) => !seen.has(id));\\r\\nconst extra = [...seen].filter((id) => !indexIds.has(id));\\r\\nif (missing.length || extra.length) {\\r\\n  throw new Error(\\r\\n    `question-id mismatch vs rc-index.json: missing=${missing.slice(0, 5)} extra=${extra.slice(0, 5)}`,\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nentries.sort((a, b) => a.questionId - b.questionId);\\r\\n\\r\\nconst out = { questionCount: entries.length, questions: entries };\\r\\nconst target = join(RC_DIR, \'rc-question-index.json\');\\r\\nwriteFileSync(target, JSON.stringify(out));\\r\\nconsole.log(`rc-search-index: wrote ${entries.length} questions to ${target}`);\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"30e284","wall_time_seconds":0.2659834,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1278,"output":"/**\\r\\n * Builds the Question jump\'s address index.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The three dropdowns on Classroom Home need to know which PrepTests, sections,\\r\\n * and question numbers exist — and nothing else. Populating them from the real\\r\\n * banks would cost the home page ~700 KB (the LR bank is 160 files, the RC\\r\\n * question index alone is 536 KB) on the page a student opens most, which is\\r\\n * exactly why `useQuestionFinderBanks` refuses to load until the finder opens.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * So this ships the addresses on their own: a few KB of numbers, no question\\r\\n * text. Picking an address still routes into the real bank, which loads then.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Source of truth is `JSON_FILES` in src/lib/questionLoader.ts, not a glob over\\r\\n * public/data — a stray file in that directory that the loader does not serve\\r\\n * would otherwise become a dropdown option that dead-ends.\\r\\n *\\r\\n *   node scripts/build-finder-address-index.mjs\\r\\n */\\r\\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from \\"node:fs\\";\\r\\nimport { resolve, dirname } from \\"node:path\\";\\r\\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \\"node:url\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nconst root = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), \\"..\\");\\r\\nconst out = resolve(root, \\"public/data/finder-address-index.json\\");\\r\\n\\r\\nconst loaderSource = readFileSync(resolve(root, \\"src/lib/questionLoader.ts\\"), \\"utf8\\");\\r\\nconst listMatch = loaderSource.match(/const JSON_FILES = \\\\[([\\\\s\\\\S]*?)\\\\n\\\\];/);\\r\\nif (!listMatch) throw new Error(\\"Could not find JSON_FILES in src/lib/questionLoader.ts\\");\\r\\nconst lrFiles = [...listMatch[1].matchAll(/\'(\\\\/data\\\\/[^\']+)\'/g)].map((m) => m[1]);\\r\\nif (lrFiles.length === 0) throw new Error(\\"JSON_FILES parsed to an empty list\\");\\r\\n\\r\\n/** pt -> section -> { lr: [qnum...], rc: [{ id, position, title }...] }\\r\\n *\\r\\n *  LR carries only question numbers: its qid is `PT{pt}-S{section}-Q{qnum}`,\\r\\n *  built the same way in `questionLoader.hashQid`, so storing it would be\\r\\n *  duplicating a formula. RC has no such formula — a passage is reached by its\\r\\n *  own id — so the ids ship. */\\r\\nconst tests = new Map();\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction section(pt, sec) {\\r\\n  if (!tests.has(pt)) tests.set(pt, new Map());\\r\\n  const sections = tests.get(pt);\\r\\n  if (!sections.has(sec)) sections.set(sec, { lr: [], rc: [] });\\r\\n  return sections.get(sec);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfor (const file of lrFiles) {\\r\\n  const questions = JSON.parse(readFileSync(resolve(root, \\"public\\", file.replace(/^\\\\//, \\"\\")), \\"utf8\\"));\\r\\n  for (const q of questions) {\\r\\n    if (q.status === \'removed\') continue;\\r\\n    const pt = Number(String(q.practiceTest ?? \\"\\").replace(/^PT/i, \\"\\"));\\r\\n    const sec = Number(q.sectionNumber);\\r\\n    const qnum = Number(q.questionNumber);\\r\\n    if (!Number.isFinite(pt) || !Number.isFinite(sec) || !Number.isFinite(qnum)) continue;\\r\\n    section(pt, sec).lr.push(qnum);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst rcIndex = JSON.parse(readFileSync(resolve(root, \\"public/data/rc/rc-index.json\\"), \\"utf8\\"));\\r\\nconst rcQuestionIndex = JSON.parse(\\r\\n  readFileSync(resolve(root, \\"public/data/rc/rc-question-index.json\\"), \\"utf8\\"),\\r\\n);\\r\\nconst rcQuestionsByPassage = new Map();\\r\\nfor (const question of rcQuestionIndex.questions ?? []) {\\r\\n  const rows = rcQuestionsByPassage.get(question.passageId) ?? [];\\r\\n  rows.push(question);\\r\\n  rcQuestionsByPassage.set(question.passageId, rows);\\r\\n}\\r\\nfor (const entry of rcIndex.preptests ?? []) {\\r\\n  const pt = Number(entry.preptest);\\r\\n  if (!Number.isFinite(pt)) continue;\\r\\n  const bySection = new Map();\\r\\n  for (const passage of entry.passages ?? []) {\\r\\n    const rows = bySection.get(passage.section) ?? [];\\r\\n    rows.push(passage);\\r\\n    bySection.set(passage.section, rows);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  for (const [rawSection, passages] of bySection) {\\r\\n    const sec = Number(rawSection);\\r\\n    if (!Number.isFinite(sec)) continue;\\r\\n    for (const passage of [...passages].sort((a, b) => a.setNumber - b.setNumber)) {\\r\\n      const questions = [...(rcQuestionsByPassage.get(passage.passageId) ?? [])]\\r\\n        .sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position)\\r\\n        .map((question) => ({\\r\\n          id: Number(question.questionId),\\r\\n          number: Number(question.position),\\r\\n        }));\\r\\n      section(pt, sec).rc.push({\\r\\n        id: Number(passage.passageId),\\r\\n        position: Number(passage.setNumber),\\r\\n        title: passage.title ?? null,\\r\\n        questions,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst payload = {\\r\\n  generatedBy: \\"scripts/build-finder-address-index.mjs\\",\\r\\n  tests: [...tests.entries()]\\r\\n    .sort((a, b) => b[0] - a[0])\\r\\n    .map(([pt, sections]) => ({\\r\\n      pt,\\r\\n      sections: [...sections.entries()]\\r\\n        .sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0])\\r\\n        .map(([sec, body]) => ({\\r\\n          section: sec,\\r\\n          lr: [...new Set(body.lr)].sort((a, b) => a - b),\\r\\n          rc: body.rc.sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position),\\r\\n        })),\\r\\n    })),\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nwriteFileSync(out, JSON.stringify(payload));\\r\\nconst bytes = readFileSync(out).length;\\r\\nconsole.log(\\r\\n  `finder-address-index.json: ${payload.tests.length} PrepTests, ` +\\r\\n    `${payload.tests.reduce((n, t) => n + t.sections.length, 0)} sections, ` +\\r\\n    `${payload.tests.reduce((n, t) => n + t.sections.reduce((m, s) => m + s.rc.length, 0), 0)} RC passages, ` +\\r\\n    `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`,\\r\\n);\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"265d05","wall_time_seconds":0.2608257,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":35620,"output":"Warning: truncated output (original token count: 35620)\\nTotal output lines: 1\\n\\n{\\"preptest\\":140,\\"passages\\":[{\\"passageId\\":14,\\"preptest\\":140,\\"section\\":4,\\"setNumber\\":1,\\"title\\":\\"Sam Gilliam\\",\\"topic\\":\\"Humanities\\",\\"format\\":\\"Standard (Non-Comparative)\\",\\"frameworks\\":[\\"Highlight Noteworthy\\"],\\"difficulty\\":2,\\"text\\":\\"African American painter Sam Gilliam (b. 1933) is internationally recognized as one of the foremost painters associated with the Washington Color School, a group of Color Field style painters practicing in Washington, D.C. during the 1950s and 1960s. The Color Field style was an important development in abstract art that emerged after the rise of abstract expressionism. It evolved from complex and minimally representational abstractions in the 1950s to totally nonrepresentational, simplified works of bright colors in the 1960s.\\\\n\\\\nGilliam’s participation in the Color Field movement was motivated in part by his reaction to the art of his African American contemporaries, much of which was strictly representational and was intended to convey explicit political statements. Gilliam found their approach to be aesthetically conservative: the message was unmistakable, he felt, and there was little room for the expression of subtlety or ambiguity or, more importantly, the exploration of new artistic territory through experimentation and innovation. For example, one of his contemporaries worked with collage, assembling disparate bits of images from popular magazines into loosely structured compositions that depicted the period’s political issues—themes such as urban life, the rural South, and African American music. Though such art was quite popular with the general public, Gilliam was impatient with its straightforward, literal approach to representation. In its place he sought an artistic form that was more expressive than a painted figure or a political slogan, more evocative of the complexity of human experience in general, and of the African American experience in particular. In this he represented a view that was then rare among African American artists.\\\\n\\\\nGilliam’s highly experimental paintings epitomized his refusal to conform to the public’s expectation that African American artists produce explicitly political art. His early experiments included pouring paint onto stained canvases and folding canvases over onto themselves. Then around 1965 Gilliam became the first painter to introduce the idea of the unsupported canvas. Partially inspired by the sight of neighbors hanging laundry on clotheslines, Gilliam began to drape huge pieces of loose canvas along floors and fold them up and down walls, even suspending them from ceilings, giving them a third dimension and therefore a sculptural quality. These efforts demonstrate a sensitivity to the texture of daily experience, as well as the ability to generate tension by juxtaposing conceptual opposites—such as surface and depth or chaos and control—to form a cohesive whole. In this way, Gilliam helped advance the notion that the deepest, hardest-to-capture emotions and tensions of being African American could not be represented directly, but were expressed more effectively through the creation of moods that would allow these emotions and tensions to be felt by all audiences.\\",\\"summaries\\":{\\"standard\\":\\"**Passage Summary**\\\\n\\\\n**Topic**\\\\n\\\\nSam Gilliam and his innovative, nonrepresentational approach to art as an African American painter associated with the Washington Color School.\\\\n\\\\n**Framework**\\\\n\\\\nHighlight Noteworthy\\\\n\\\\n**Main Point**\\\\n\\\\nSam Gilliam set himself apart from his contemporaries by rejecting literal, representational, and political art, instead favoring experimental, abstract works that evoke complex emotions and experiences—especially those of African Americans—without directly depicting them. **The core of this main point appears at the end of the second paragraph: “Gilliam helped advance the notion that the deepest, hardest-to-capture emotions and tensions of being African American could not be represented directly, but were expressed more effectively through the creation of moods that would allow these emotions and tensions to be felt by all audiences.”**\\\\n\\\\n**P1: Introduction to Sam Gilliam and the Washington Color School**\\\\n\\\\nGilliam is introduced as a prominent figure in abstract art, specifically tied to the Washington Color School. The paragraph gives us some background on the Color Field style and sets up where…33420 tokens truncated…prompt\\":\\"The author mentions the fact that we rarely focus our eyes on mirrors (near the end of the third paragraph) primarily in order to\\",\\"correctAnswer\\":\\"C\\",\\"hasWrittenExplanation\\":true,\\"explanationBlocks\\":[{\\"heading\\":\\"Anticipate\\",\\"body\\":\\"This is a Local Purpose question, which uses language like *serves to / primarily to / in order to*. text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"node --input-type=module -e \"import fs from 'node:fs'; const d='public/data/rc/'; const i=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(d+'rc-index.json')); const m=new Map(i.preptests.flatMap(t=>t.passages.map(p=>[p.passageId,{pt:t.preptest,...p}]))); const errors=[]; for(const f of fs.readdirSync(d).filter(x=>/^RC-PT\\d+\\.json$/.test(x))){ const b=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(d+f)); for(const p of b.passages){const x=m.get(p.passageId);for(const k of ['section','setNumber'])if(p[k]!==x[k])errors.push({id:p.passageId,k,bank:p[k],index:x[k]}); const a=p.questions.map(q=>q.questionId).sort((a,b)=>a-b); const z=[...x.questionIds].sort((a,b)=>a-b);if(JSON.stringify(a)!==JSON.stringify(z))errors.push({id:p.passageId,k:'questionIds'});}} console.log(JSON.stringify(errors));\" ",workdir:"C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu",max_output_tokens:1500}));

## assistant

**Partial compliance. One verifier correction is needed before closing ticket01.**

**[P2] The official-address check trusts another index rather than the bank.** In `scripts/verify-rc-search-index.mjs:68–80`, expected section and passage position come from `rc-index.json`; the corresponding `RC-PT*.json` passage’s `section` and `setNumber` are ignored. A stale section/passage address shared by both generated indexes can therefore pass.

I independently demonstrated this without modifying files: loading PT140’s first passage with an in-memory `section=99` substitution still produced exit0 and “official-address sets match exactly.” The current bank and `rc-index.json` do agree, so this is a guard defect, not evidence that current output addresses are wrong.

**Required correction:** compare bank passage metadata and question ownership with `rc-index.json`, construct expected official addresses from the bank, and add regressions for section/set-number disagreements and passage question-ID swaps.

Otherwise, the reviewed implementation covers the primary requested repair:

- Search generator uses `fileURLToPath`, fixing Windows URL pathname handling.
- Prebuild runs bank generation, RC search, finder generation, then verification.
- Verifier compares complete question-ID sets, rejects duplicated question identities, checks output addresses/passage IDs and detects stale search content.
- Existing four tests cover valid output, current PT140 completeness, missing records and duplicate search records.

Coverage gaps: the tests do not exercise finder corruption, wrong addresses, Windows generator execution or the metadata disagreement above. The current-bank assertion also hardcodes2150; exact equality should remain the durable invariant when the bank legitimately changes.

No files modified, generators/builds run, pushes or deployments performed.

## assistant

**Spec re-review: no remaining material defect found. The earlier P2 issue is fixed.**

In `scripts/verify-rc-search-index.mjs:31–79`, expected passage identities, section/set addresses and question ownership now come directly from `RC-PT<n>.json`. The verifier independently checks `rc-index.json` against that bank, including metadata and exact per-passage question ownership/order (`:84–136`). Search records check both address and passage owner (`:164–166`); finder records check passage metadata, duplicates and question addresses (`:180–216`).

I repeated the earlier failing scenario entirely in memory: changing PT140’s first bank passage from section4 to section99 now produces **exit1**, with actionable passage and question-address diagnostics. No files were modified.

The expanded regression file adds:

- Duplicate finder passage rejection.
- RC index section and set-number disagreement rejection.
- Question-ID swaps between passages, even when the overall ID set stays unchanged.

These tests directly address the previous review finding. The combined diff still contains only build wiring, generator/verifier tests and generated lookup artifacts; no RC source or answer-key files are included.

Remaining coverage observations are nonblocking: the real-bank test hardcodes2150, and there is no dedicated Windows path execution regression. The generator itself uses the correct native-path conversion and build ordering remains appropriate.

I did not run tests that create temporary files, generators, builds, push or deployment. This review establishes specification compliance in source and independently confirms the repaired authority check; it does not establish production acceptance.

It is ignored while the student is typing somewhere else,\\r\\n     so it cannot steal a keystroke from a real field.\\r\\n\\r\\n     \\"/\\" opens it too, the convention everywhere else a search box lives behind\\r\\n     a key. It only opens, it does not toggle: \\"/\\" while the panel is open is a\\r\\n     character someone is trying to type. */\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {\\r\\n      const target = event.target as HTMLElement | null;\\r\\n      const tag = target?.tagName;\\r\\n      const typing = tag === \\"INPUT\\" || tag === \\"TEXTAREA\\" || target?.isContentEditable === true;\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (event.key === \\"/\\" && !event.metaKey && !event.ctrlKey && !event.altKey) {\\r\\n        if (open || typing) return;\\r\\n        event.preventDefault();\\r\\n        setOpen(true);\\r\\n        return;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (event.key.toLowerCase() !== \\"k\\" || !(event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey)) return;\\r\\n      if (!open && typing) return;\\r\\n      event.preventDefault();\\r\\n      setOpen((value) => !value);\\r\\n    };\\r\\n    window.addEventListener(\\"keydown\\", onKey);\\r\\n    return () => window.removeEventListener(\\"keydown\\", onKey);\\r\\n  }, [open]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"qf\\" data-tone={tone}>\\r\\n      {/* ONE trigger, not one per breakpoint. \\"Section\\" : \\"Pick a test\\"}</option>\\r\\n621:           {sections.map((value) => (\\r\\n622:             <option key={value} value={value}>\\r\\n623:               Section {value}\\r\\n624:             </option>\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"51199b","wall_time_seconds":0.3120563,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":919,"output":"/**\\r\\n * Loader for the static RC bank in public/data/rc/.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The index is small and loads once; per-PrepTest files load on demand and stay\\r\\n * cached in memory for the life of the page.\\r\\n */\\r\\nimport type {\\r\\n  RcIndex,\\r\\n  RcIndexPassage,\\r\\n  RcPassage,\\r\\n  RcPreptestFile,\\r\\n  RcQuestionIndex,\\r\\n} from \'./rcTypes\';\\r\\n\\r\\nlet indexPromise: Promise<RcIndex> | null = null;\\r\\nlet questionIndexPromise: Promise<RcQuestionIndex> | null = null;\\r\\nconst preptestCache = new Map<number, Promise<RcPreptestFile>>();\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function fetchJson<T>(url: string): Promise<T> {\\r\\n  const res = await fetch(url);\\r\\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to load ${url} (${res.status})`);\\r\\n  return (await res.json()) as T;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Loads (and caches) the passage index. Safe to call from anywhere. */\\r\\nexport function loadRcIndex(): Promise<RcIndex> {\\r\\n  if (!indexPromise) {\\r\\n    indexPromise = fetchJson<RcIndex>(\'/data/rc/rc-index.json\').catch((err) => {\\r\\n      indexPromise = null; // let a later call retry rather than caching a failure\\r\\n      throw err;\\r\\n    });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return indexPromise;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Loads (and caches) the flat question search index used by `/rc/search`. */\\r\\nexport function loadRcQuestionIndex(): Promise<RcQuestionIndex> {\\r\\n  if (!questionIndexPromise) {\\r\\n    questionIndexPromise = fetchJson<RcQuestionIndex>(\'/data/rc/rc-question-index.json\').catch(\\r\\n      (err) => {\\r\\n        questionIndexPromise = null; // let a later call retry rather than caching a failure\\r\\n        throw err;\\r\\n      },\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return questionIndexPromise;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Loads (and caches) one PrepTest\'s passages and questions. */\\r\\nexport function loadPreptest(preptest: number): Promise<RcPreptestFile> {\\r\\n  const cached = preptestCache.get(preptest);\\r\\n  if (cached) return cached;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const promise = fetchJson<RcPreptestFile>(`/data/rc/RC-PT${preptest}.json`).catch((err) => {\\r\\n    preptestCache.delete(preptest);\\r\\n    throw err;\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  preptestCache.set(preptest, promise);\\r\\n  return promise;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Finds which PrepTest a passage belongs to, then loads that passage in full. */\\r\\nexport async function loadPassage(passageId: number): Promise<RcPassage | null> {\\r\\n  const index = await loadRcIndex();\\r\\n  const pt = index.preptests.find((t) => t.passages.some((p) => p.passageId === passageId));\\r\\n  if (!pt) return null;\\r\\n  const file = await loadPreptest(pt.preptest);\\r\\n  return file.passages.find((p) => p.passageId === passageId) ?? null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Loads several passages, one PrepTest fetch per distinct test, order preserved. */\\r\\nexport async function loadPassages(passageIds: number[]): Promise<RcPassage[]> {\\r\\n  const index = await loadRcIndex();\\r\\n  const ptByPassage = new Map<number, number>();\\r\\n  for (const pt of index.preptests) {\\r\\n    for (const p of pt.passages) ptByPassage.set(p.passageId, pt.preptest);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const needed = [...new Set(passageIds.map((id) => ptByPassage.get(id)).filter(Boolean))];\\r\\n  const files = await Promise.all(needed.map((n) => loadPreptest(n as number)));\\r\\n  const byId = new Map<number, RcPassage>();\\r\\n  for (const f of files) for (const p of f.passages) byId.set(p.passageId, p);\\r\\n  return passageIds.map((id) => byId.get(id)).filter(Boolean) as RcPassage[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Every passage in the index, flattened, with its PrepTest attached. */\\r\\nexport async function loadAllIndexPassages(): Promise<(RcIndexPassage & { preptest: number })[]> {\\r\\n  const index = await loadRcIndex();\\r\\n  return index.preptests.flatMap((pt) => pt.passages.map((p) => ({ ...p, preptest: pt.preptest })));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport {\\r\\n  RC_TOPICS,\\r\\n  RC_FORMATS,\\r\\n  RC_FRAMEWORKS,\\r\\n  RC_QUESTION_TYPES,\\r\\n  RC_QUESTION_TYPE_FAMILIES,\\r\\n  RC_FEATURES,\\r\\n} from \'./rcTypes\';\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"a0a11d","wall_time_seconds":0.3694054,"exit_code":1,"original_token_count":261,"output":"rg: src/pages/Foyer.tsx: The system cannot find the file specified. Pushed, not replaced, so Back returns to the section they\\r\\n     came from.\\r\\n\\r\\n     The switch is unconditional. A half-built LR set is discarded without a\\r\\n     prompt — Joshua\'s call, 2026-08-26: a flip that stops to ask is not a\\r\\n     flip. */\\r\\n  const changeSection = React.useCallback(\\r\\n    (next: DrillSection) => {\\r\\n      rememberSection(next);\\r\\n      setSearchParams({ [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: next }, { replace: false });\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    [setSearchParams],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  const openAnalytics = React.useCallback(\\r\\n    (tab: string = \\"overview\\") => {\\r\\n      setSearchParams(\\r\\n        { [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: section, stage: \\"analytics\\", tab },\\r\\n        { replace: false },\\r\\n      );\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    [section, setSearchParams],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  const setAnalyticsTab = React.useCallback(\\r\\n    (tab: string) => {\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"1f3d34","wall_time_seconds":0.4033001,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":728,"output":"import * as React from \\"react\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface FinderAddressRcPassage {\\r\\n  id: number;\\r\\n  /** The passage\'s position in its section — RC\'s Position, not a Set. */\\r\\n  position: number;\\r\\n  title: string | null;\\r\\n  questions: Array<{\\r\\n    /** Internal question id used to deep-link the RC player. */\\r\\n    id: number;\\r\\n    /** Official question number across the full section. */\\r\\n    number: number;\\r\\n  }>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface FinderAddressSection {\\r\\n  section: number;\\r\\n  /** LR question numbers, ascending. Empty on an RC section. */\\r\\n  lr: number[];\\r\\n  /** RC passages, by position. Empty on an LR section. */\\r\\n  rc: FinderAddressRcPassage[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface FinderAddressTest {\\r\\n  pt: number;\\r\\n  sections: FinderAddressSection[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface FinderAddressIndex {\\r\\n  tests: FinderAddressTest[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The addresses, and only the addresses.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Built by scripts/build-finder-address-index.mjs. This exists so the three\\r\\n * dropdowns can be populated and usable the instant Home paints: the real banks\\r\\n * are ~700 KB and `useQuestionFinderBanks` refuses to touch them until the\\r\\n * search panel is actually opened, which is a rule worth keeping. Picking an\\r\\n * address here routes into the bank, and the bank loads then.\\r\\n */\\r\\nlet cached: Promise<FinderAddressIndex> | null = null;\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction load(): Promise<FinderAddressIndex> {\\r\\n  if (!cached) {\\r\\n    /* Promise.resolve().then, not a bare fetch(): under jsdom and any SSR pass\\r\\n       `fetch` can be missing entirely, and a synchronous throw here would\\r\\n       escape the caller\'s effect instead of landing in the catch below. */\\r\\n    cached = Promise.resolve()\\r\\n      .then(() => fetch(\\"/data/finder-address-index.json\\"))\\r\\n      .then((res) => {\\r\\n        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`finder address index ${res.status}`);\\r\\n        return res.json() as Promise<FinderAddressIndex>;\\r\\n      })\\r\\n      .catch((err) => {\\r\\n        cached = null; // let a later mount retry rather than caching the failure\\r\\n        throw err;\\r\\n      });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return cached;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function useFinderAddressIndex(enabled = true) {\\r\\n  const [tests, setTests] = React.useState<FinderAddressTest[]>([]);\\r\\n  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(enabled);\\r\\n  const [failed, setFailed] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n  const [attempt, setAttempt] = React.useState(0);\\r\\n\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (!enabled) return;\\r\\n    let cancelled = false;\\r\\n    setLoading(true);\\r\\n    setFailed(false);\\r\\n    load()\\r\\n      .then((index) => {\\r\\n        if (cancelled) return;\\r\\n        setTests(index.tests ?? []);\\r\\n        setLoading(false);\\r\\n      })\\r\\n      .catch(() => {\\r\\n        if (cancelled) return;\\r\\n        setFailed(true);\\r\\n        setLoading(false);\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    return () => {\\r\\n      cancelled = true;\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }, [enabled, attempt]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return { tests, loading, failed, retry: () => setAttempt((value) => value + 1) };\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

**Ticket02 specification review: two concrete issues remain.**

1. **[P2] Bank changes leave a stale address that returns after reopening.** `src/components/questionfinder/QuestionFinder.tsx:571–581` resets only the child selectors when the bank changes, while the parent query retains the old address. Selecting RC PT140 S4 Q3 and switching to LR leaves the RC address in the query. Closing and reopening rehydrates section4/question3 into LR selectors, despite section4 not being a valid LR choice. Reset or validate the shared address on genuine bank changes; preserve only addresses valid in the new bank. Add switch-and-reopen regressions.

2. **[P2] Dialog keyboard handling overrides the native selectors.** `src/components/questionfinder/QuestionFinder.tsx:201–227` intercepts ArrowUp/Down and Enter for every focused descendant. Test and Section remain native `<select>` elements (`:588–624`), so their keyboard events bubble into this handler, which prevents default selector movement and instead changes the highlighted search result or selects that result. Restrict search-result keyboard handling to the search field/results surface, leaving selector keys to their own controls. Test actual keyboard selection and focus behavior.

Otherwise, the source implements the main ticket02 structure: landing and sidebar reach the same finder component, selections no longer launch question sessions, the final action stays disabled, PT140’s questions are grouped with official ranges and accessible group labels, and explicit Retry controls exist.

The current tests cover close/reopen retention, grouping, unique RC-section selection and no-navigation selection. They do not prove the two failing paths above or signed-in responsive/keyboard acceptance.

No files modified, builds/generators run, pushes, deployments or ticket03 work performed.

Added four regressions in `QuestionFinder.test.tsx`:

- RC address clears on LR switch and remains cleared after reopening.
- Native Test/Section keyboard events remain unprevented and leave search unchanged.
- Portaled passage menu retains dark finder token scope.
- All→RC cannot auto-select a stale test.

Updated the existing Enter test to target the search textbox.

Focused verification: **22/22 tests passed**. Only the test file changed.

Keyed by passage so they restore on any\\r\\n * future encounter with that passage, in this session or a later one.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function useRcHighlights(passageIds: number[]) {\\r\\n  const [byPassage, setByPassage] = React.useState<Map<number, RcHighlight[]>>(new Map());\\r\\n  const [color, setColor] = React.useState<RcHighlightColor>(\'yellow\');\\r\\n  const [loaded, setLoaded] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Mirrors `byPassage`, but updated synchronously on every write. Reading the\\r\\n   * state variable inside the mutators would capture whatever the last render\\r\\n   * closed over, so two marks made between renders — which is simply someone\\r\\n   * highlighting quickly — would compute from the same base and the first\\r\\n   * would be lost.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const latest = React.useRef<Map<number, RcHighlight[]>>(new Map());\\r\\n\\r\\n  const key = passageIds.join(\',\');\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    let cancelled = false;\\r\\n    if (passageIds.length === 0) return;\\r\\n    loadAnnotations(passageIds)\\r\\n      .then((map) => {\\r\\n        if (!cancelled) {\\r\\n          latest.current = map;\\r\\n          setByPassage(map);\\r\\n          setLoaded(true);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      })\\r\\n      .catch(() => {\\r\\n        // Highlights are an aid, not the drill. A load failure must not block it.\\r\\n        if (!cancelled) setLoaded(true);\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    return () => {\\r\\n      cancelled = true;\\r\\n    };\\r\\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\\r\\n  }, [key]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const forPassage = React.useCallback(\\r\\n    (passageId: number) => byPassage.get(passageId) ?? [],\\r\\n    [byPassage],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  const write = React.useCallback((passageId: number, next: RcHighlight[]) => {\\r\\n    const map = new Map(latest.current);\\r\\n    map.set(passageId, next);\\r\\n    latest.current = map;\\r\\n    setByPassage(map);\\r\\n    saveAnnotations(passageId, next);\\r\\n  }, []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const add = React.useCallback(\\r\\n    (\\r\\n      passageId: number,\\r\\n      target: RcHighlightTarget,\\r\\n      start: number,\\r\\n      end: number,\\r\\n      /** Passed in by the popover so picking a swatch applies that color now. */\\r\\n      chosen: RcHighlightColor = color,\\r\\n      kind: RcAnnotationKind = \'highlight\',\\r\\n    ) => {\\r\\n      if (end <= start) return;\\r\\n      const existing = latest.current.get(passageId) ?? [];\\r\\n      // Same target + same kind is one layer. A highlight and an underline can\\r\\n      // sit on the same words, so they never interact.\\r\\n      const sameLayer = (h: RcHighlight) =>\\r\\n        h.target === target && (h.kind ?? \'highlight\') === kind;\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Marking over the same color extends one continuous span rather than\\r\\n      // leaving a seam. Repeat until nothing new is absorbed: swallowing one\\r\\n      // neighbour can bring the next into reach.\\r\\n      let lo = start;\\r\\n      let hi = end;\\r\\n      for (let grew = true; grew; ) {\\r\\n        grew = false;\\r\\n        for (const h of existing) {\\r\\n          if (!sameLayer(h) || h.color !== chosen) continue;\\r\\n          if (h.end < lo || h.start > hi) continue;\\r\\n          if (h.start < lo || h.end > hi) {\\r\\n            lo = Math.min(lo, h.start);\\r\\n            hi = Math.max(hi, h.end);\\r\\n            grew = true;\\r\\n          }\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Marking over a different color trims the old span around the new one\\r\\n      // instead of discarding it, so highlighting three words inside a\\r\\n      // sentence keeps the rest of that sentence.\\r\\n      const next: RcHighlight[] = [];\\r\\n      for (const h of existing) {\\r\\n        if (!sameLayer(h)) {\\r\\n          next.push(h);\\r\\n          continue;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        if (h.color === chosen && h.end >= lo && h.start <= hi) continue;\\r\\n        if (h.end <= lo || h.start >= hi) {\\r\\n          next.push(h);\\r\\n          continue;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        if (h.start < lo) next.push({ ...h, end: lo });\\r\\n        if (h.end > hi) next.push({ ...h, start: hi });\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      next.push({ target, start: lo, end: hi, color: chosen, kind });\\r\\n      write(passageId, next);\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    [color, write],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  const removeAt = React.useCallback(\\r\\n    (passageId: number, target: RcHighlightTarget, offset: number) => {\\r\\n      const existing = latest.current.get(passageId) ?? [];\\r\\n      write(\\r\\n        passageId,\\r\\n        existing.filter((h) => !(h.target === target && offset >= h.start && offset < h.end)),\\r\\n      );\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    [write],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  /** Eraser drag: clear every annotation the swept range touches. */\\r\\n  const removeRange = React.useCallback(\\r\\n    (passageId: number, target: RcHighlightTarget, start: number, end: number) => {\\r\\n      const existing = latest.current.get(passageId) ?? [];\\r\\n      write(\\r\\n        passageId,\\r\\n        existing.filter((h) => h.target !== target || h.end <= start || h.start >= end),\\r\\n      );\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    [write],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  return { forPassage, add, removeAt, removeRange, color, setColor, loaded };\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"610b2a","wall_time_seconds":0.3667567,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":4037,"output":"/**\\r\\n * Supabase reads and writes for RC progress: sessions, attempts, annotations.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Content is static JSON (see rcBank.ts). Run supabase/migrations/20260806000000_rc_drilling.sql.\',\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    this.name = \'RcTablesMissingError\';\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction raise(error: { code?: string; message?: string } | null): void {\\r\\n  if (!error) return;\\r\\n  if (error.code === MISSING_TABLE) throw new RcTablesMissingError();\\r\\n  throw new Error(error.message ?? \'Unknown Supabase error\');\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Confirms the progress tables exist. Call once on player/hub boot. */\\r\\nexport async function checkRcTables(): Promise<true> {\\r\\n  const { error } = await rcDb.from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE).select(\'id\').limit(1);\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n  return true;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function requireUserId(): Promise<string> {\\r\\n  const { data } = await rcDb.auth.getUser();\\r\\n  const id = data?.user?.id;\\r\\n  if (!id) throw new Error(\'You need to be signed in to drill.\');\\r\\n  return id;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function toSession(row: RcDrillSessionRow): RcDrillSession {\\r\\n  const config = row.config as RcDrillConfig & { timerSeconds?: number | null };\\r\\n  if (!config || typeof config !== \'object\' || Array.isArray(config)) throw new Error(\'The saved reading section settings could not be verified.\');\\r\\n  const protectedId = (row as RcDrillSessionRow & { timed_section_sitting_id?: string | null }).timed_section_sitting_id ?? config.timedSectionSittingId;\\r\\n  const protectedTestId = config.practiceTestRunId ?? row.practice_test_run_id;\\r\\n  // The protected writer stores one section budget, unlike legacy passage clocks.\\r\\n  // Adapt that acknowledged shape for the existing read-only report and review.\\r\\n  const timing = config.timing ?? ((protectedId || protectedTestId) && config.timerSeconds === null ? { mode: \'unlimited\' as const }\\r\\n    : (protectedId || protectedTestId) && typeof config.timerSeconds === \'number\' && Number.isFinite(config.timerSeconds) && config.timerSeconds > 0 && row.passage_ids?.length\\r\\n      ? { mode: \'per_passage_budget\' as const, secondsPerPassage: config.timerSeconds / row.passage_ids.length } : undefined);\\r\\n  if (!timing || ![\'unlimited\', \'per_passage_budget\'].includes(timing.mode)) throw new Error(\'The saved reading section timing could not be verified.\');\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    id: row.id,\\r\\n    userId: row.user_id,\\r\\n    config: {\\r\\n      ...config,\\r\\n      timing,\\r\\n      timedSectionSittingId: protectedId,\\r\\n      practiceTestRunId: config.practiceTestRunId ?? row.practice_test_run_id ?? undefined,\\r\\n      practiceTestLegIndex: config.practiceTestLegIndex ?? row.practice_test_leg ?? undefined,\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    passageIds: row.passage_ids ?? [],\\r\\n    status: row.status as RcSessionStatus,\\r\\n    excludedFromAnalytics: row.excluded_from_analytics,\\r\\n    startedAt: row.started_at,\\r\\n    completedAt: row.completed_at,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction toAttempt(row: RcAttemptRow): RcAttempt {\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    id: row.id,\\r\\n    sessionId: row.session_id,\\r\\n    userId: row.user_id,\\r\\n    questionId: row.question_id,\\r\\n    passageId: row.passage_id,\\r\\n    selected: (row.selected as RcLetter | null) ?? null,\\r\\n    isCorrect: row.is_correct,\\r\\n    timeMs: row.time_ms,\\r\\n    flagged: row.flagged,\\r\\n    eliminations: (row.eliminations ?? []) as RcLetter[],\\r\\n    isFirstAttempt: row.is_first_attempt,\\r\\n    createdAt: row.created_at,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function assertActivePracticeTestSession(sessionId: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const session = await getSession(sessionId);\\r\\n  if (!session) throw new Error(\'That practice section could not be found.\');\\r\\n  if (session.config.practiceTestRunId && !await isActivePracticeTestDevice(session.userId, session.config.practiceTestRunId)) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'This practice test is active on another device.\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- sessions */\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function createSession(\\r\\n  config: RcDrillConfig,\\r\\n  passageIds: number[],\\r\\n  request?: { id: string; actorId: string },\\r\\n): Promise<RcDrillSession> {\\r\\n  const userId = await requireUserId();\\r\\n  if (request && request.actorId !== userId) throw new Error(\'The signed-in account changed.\');\\r\\n  if (config.practiceTestRunId && !await isActivePracticeTestDevice(userId, config.practiceTestRunId)) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'This practice test is active on another device.\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (await requireUserId() !== userId) throw new Error(\'The signed-in account changed.\');\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .insert({\\r\\n      ...(request ? { id: request.id } : {}),\\r\\n      user_id: userId,\\r\\n      config,\\r\\n      passage_ids: passageIds,\\r\\n      practice_test_run_id: config.practiceTestRunId ?? null,\\r\\n      practice_test_leg: config.practiceTestLegIndex ?? null,\\r\\n    })\\r\\n    .select()\\r\\n    .single();\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n  if (await requireUserId() !== userId) throw new Error(\'The signed-in account changed.\');\\r\\n  const saved = toSession(data as RcDrillSessionRow);\\r\\n  if (saved.userId !== userId || request && saved.id !== request.id) throw new Error(\'The reading drill save could not be verified.\');\\r\\n  return saved;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function getSession(id: string, expectedActorId?: string): Promise<RcDrillSession | null> {\\r\\n  if (expectedActorId && await requireUserId() !== expectedActorId) throw new Error(\'The signed-in account changed.\');\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb.from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE).select().eq(\'id\', id).maybeSingle();\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n  if (expectedActorId && await requireUserId() !== expectedActorId) throw new Error(\'The signed-in account changed.\');\\r\\n  return data ? toSession(data as RcDrillSessionRow) : null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function listSessions(): Promise<RcDrillSession[]> {\\r\\n  const userId = await requireUserId();\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .select()\\r\\n    // Admin read access includes student work; this list is the personal history.\\r\\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\r\\n    .order(\'started_at\', { ascending: false });\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n  return ((data ?? []) as RcDrillSessionRow[]).map(toSession);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst clockWriteChains = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Save spent time and, on navigation, the account-acknowledged question position. */\\r\\nexport function saveSessionClock(id: string, elapsedSeconds: number, currentQuestionIndex?: number): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const previous = clockWriteChains.get(id) ?? Promise.resolve();\\r\\n  const write = previous.catch(() => undefined).then(() => writeSessionClock(id, elapsedSeconds, currentQuestionIndex));\\r\\n  clockWriteChains.set(id, write);\\r\\n  void write.finally(() => {\\r\\n    if (clockWriteChains.get(id) === write) clockWriteChains.delete(id);\\r\\n  }).catch(() => undefined);\\r\\n  return write;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function writeSessionClock(id: string, elapsedSeconds: number, currentQuestionIndex?: number): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  if (currentQuestionIndex !== undefined && (!Number.isSafeInteger(currentQuestionIndex) || currentQuestionIndex < 0)) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'That question position could not be saved.\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const userId = await requireUserId();\\r\\n  const session = await getSession(id);\\r\\n  if (!session || session.userId !== userId || session.status !== \'in_progress\') {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'The section is no longer available to save.\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  await assertActivePracticeTestSession(id);\\r\\n  const seconds = Math.max(session.config.clockElapsedSeconds ?? 0, Math.floor(elapsedSeconds));\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .update({\\r\\n      config: {\\r\\n        ...session.config,\\r\\n        clockElapsedSeconds: seconds,\\r\\n        ...(currentQuestionIndex === undefined ? {} : { currentQuestionIndex }),\\r\\n        lastActiveAt: new Date().toISOString(),\\r\\n      },\\r\\n    })\\r\\n    .eq(\'id\', id)\\r\\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'status\', \'in_progress\')\\r\\n    .select(\'id\')\\r\\n    .maybeSingle();\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n  if (!data) throw new Error(\'The section clock did not reach your account.\');\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function completeSession(id: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const userId = await requireUserId();\\r\\n  const session = await getSession(id);\\r\\n  if (!session || session.userId !== userId) throw new Error(\'This section is not available.\');\\r\\n  if (session.status === \'completed\') return;\\r\\n  if (session.status !== \'in_progress\') throw new Error(\'This section cannot be completed.\');\\r\\n  await assertActivePracticeTestSession(id);\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .update({ status: \'completed\', completed_at: new Date().toISOString() })\\r\\n    .eq(\'id\', id)\\r\\n    .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'status\', \'in_progress\')\\r\\n    .select(\'id\')\\r\\n    .maybeSingle();\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n  if (!data) {\\r\\n    const latest = await getSession(id);\\r\\n    if (latest?.userId === userId && latest.status === \'completed\') return;\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'The completed section did not reach your account.\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function setSessionExcluded(id: string, excluded: boolean): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const { error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .update({ excluded_from_analytics: excluded })\\r\\n    .eq(\'id\', id);\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function deleteSession(id: string): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const { error } = await rcDb.from(RC_SESSIONS_TABLE).delete().eq(\'id\', id);\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- attempts */\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Writes one attempt row. The first write for a question within a session is the\\r\\n * first attempt; every later write for that same question, review re-answers\\r\\n * included, is a new row flagged `is_first_attempt: false`. Existing rows are\\r\\n * never updated, so the original answer stays intact.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function recordAttempt(attempt: RcAttempt): Promise<RcAttempt> {\\r\\n  const userId = attempt.userId ?? (await requireUserId());\\r\\n  await assertActivePracticeTestSession(attempt.sessionId);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const existingAttempt = async (): Promise<RcAttempt | null> => {\\r\\n    if (!attempt.id) return null;\\r\\n    const { data, error } = await rcDb.from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE).select()\\r\\n      .eq(\'id\', attempt.id).eq(\'user_id\', userId).maybeSingle();\\r\\n    raise(error);\\r\\n    if (!data) return null;\\r\\n    const saved = toAttempt(data as RcAttemptRow);\\r\\n    if (saved.sessionId !== attempt.sessionId || saved.questionId !== attempt.questionId\\r\\n      || saved.passageId !== attempt.passageId || saved.selected !== attempt.selected) {\\r\\n      throw new Error(\'The saved answer ID belongs to different work.\');\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return saved;\\r\\n  };\\r\\n  // The client keeps this UUID in its device backup. A successful insert whose\\r\\n  // response was lost must be acknowledged as the same row on retry.\\r\\n  const alreadySaved = await existingAttempt();\\r\\n  if (alreadySaved) return alreadySaved;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: prior, error: priorError } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .select(\'id\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'session_id\', attempt.sessionId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'question_id\', attempt.questionId)\\r\\n    .limit(1);\\r\\n  raise(priorError);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const isFirstAttempt = (prior ?? []).length === 0;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .insert({\\r\\n      ...(attempt.id ? { id: attempt.id } : {}),\\r\\n      session_id: attempt.sessionId,\\r\\n      user_id: userId,\\r\\n      question_id: attempt.questionId,\\r\\n      passage_id: attempt.passageId,\\r\\n      selected: attempt.selected,\\r\\n      is_correct: attempt.isCorrect,\\r\\n      time_ms: attempt.timeMs,\\r\\n      flagged: attempt.flagged,\\r\\n      eliminations: attempt.eliminations,\\r\\n      is_first_attempt: isFirstAttempt,\\r\\n    })\\r\\n    .select()\\r\\n    .single();\\r\\n  if (error?.code === \'23505\') {\\r\\n    const saved = await existingAttempt();\\r\\n    if (saved) return saved;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n  return toAttempt(data as RcAttemptRow);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function getSessionAttempts(sessionId: string): Promise<RcAttempt[]> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .select()\\r\\n    .eq(\'session_id\', sessionId)\\r\\n    .order(\'created_at\', { ascending: true });\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n  return ((data ?? []) as RcAttemptRow[]).map(toAttempt);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Sets the flag on every stored attempt for a question in a session. */\\r\\nexport async function setAttemptFlag(\\r\\n  sessionId: string,\\r\\n  questionId: number,\\r\\n  flagged: boolean,\\r\\n): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  await assertActivePracticeTestSession(sessionId);\\r\\n  const { error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .update({ flagged })\\r\\n    .eq(\'session_id\', sessionId)\\r\\n    .eq(\'question_id\', questionId);\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Per-question aggregate over the student\'s whole history, used by the hub\'s\\r\\n * status filters and by adaptive selection.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function getQuestionHistory(userId?: string): Promise<RcQuestionHistory> {\\r\\n  const uid = userId ?? (await requireUserId());\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_ATTEMPTS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .select(\'question_id, is_correct, created_at\')\\r\\n    .eq(\'user_id\', uid)\\r\\n    .order(\'created_at\', { ascending: true });\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const history: RcQuestionHistory = new Map();\\r\\n  for (const row of (data ?? []) as Pick<\\r\\n    RcAttemptRow,\\r\\n    \'question_id\' | \'is_correct\' | \'created_at\'\\r\\n  >[]) {\\r\\n    const existing = history.get(row.question_id) ?? {\\r\\n      questionId: row.question_id,\\r\\n      attempts: 0,\\r\\n      everIncorrect: false,\\r\\n      incorrectOnLastTake: false,\\r\\n      incorrectCount: 0,\\r\\n      lastSeenAt: null as string | null,\\r\\n    };\\r\\n    const wrong = row.is_correct === false;\\r\\n    existing.attempts += 1;\\r\\n    if (wrong) {\\r\\n      existing.everIncorrect = true;\\r\\n      existing.incorrectCount += 1;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    // rows arrive oldest first, so the last one seen decides the latest take\\r\\n    existing.incorrectOnLastTake = wrong;\\r\\n    existing.lastSeenAt = row.created_at;\\r\\n    history.set(row.question_id, existing);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return history;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------- annotations */\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function loadAnnotations(\\r\\n  passageIds: number[],\\r\\n): Promise<Map<number, RcHighlight[]>> {\\r\\n  const result = new Map<number, RcHighlight[]>();\\r\\n  if (passageIds.length === 0) return result;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await rcDb\\r\\n    .from(RC_ANNOTATIONS_TABLE)\\r\\n    .select(\'passage_id, highlights\')\\r\\n    .in(\'passage_id\', passageIds);\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n\\r\\n  for (const row of (data ?? []) as { passage_id: number; highlights: unknown }[]) {\\r\\n    result.set(row.passage_id, (row.highlights ?? []) as RcHighlight[]);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return result;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst saveTimers = new Map<number, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Debounced by one second per passage, so dragging out a highlight writes once. */\\r\\nexport function saveAnnotations(passageId: number, highlights: RcHighlight[]): void {\\r\\n  const pending = saveTimers.get(passageId);\\r\\n  if (pending) clearTimeout(pending);\\r\\n  saveTimers.set(\\r\\n    passageId,\\r\\n    setTimeout(() => {\\r\\n      saveTimers.delete(passageId);\\r\\n      void saveAnnotationsNow(passageId, highlights);\\r\\n    }, 1000),\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function saveAnnotationsNow(\\r\\n  passageId: number,\\r\\n  highlights: RcHighlight[],\\r\\n): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const userId = await requireUserId();\\r\\n  const { error } = await rcDb.from(RC_ANNOTATIONS_TABLE).upsert(\\r\\n    {\\r\\n      user_id: userId,\\r\\n      passage_id: passageId,\\r\\n      highlights,\\r\\n      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    { onConflict: \'user_id,passage_id\' },\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  raise(error);\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu/src/features/rc/hooks/useRcFlagPersistence.ts\n+import * as React from 'react';\n+import { setAttemptFlag } from '../api/rcProgress';\n+import type { RcAnnotationSaveStatus } from './useRcHighlights';\n+\n+interface Options {\n+  actorId?: string | null;\n+  sessionId?: string | null;\n+  enabled: boolean;\n+  recordedQuestionIds: number[];\n+}\n+\n+interface Intent { flagged: boolean; version: number }\n+interface Owner {\n+  actor: string; session: string; cancelled: boolean;\n+  pending: Map<number, Intent>; saving: Map<number, number>; chains: Map<number, Promise<void>>;\n+}\n+const prefixFor = (actor: string, session: string) => `rc:flags:pending:${encodeURIComponent(actor)}:${encodeURIComponent(session)}:`;\n+const keyFor = (owner: Owner, questionId: number) => `${prefixFor(owner.actor, owner.session)}${questionId}`;\n+\n+/** Personal drill flags only. The player owns answer creation and source restrictions. */\n+export function useRcFlagPersistence({ actorId, sessionId, enabled, recordedQuestionIds }: Options) {\n+  const context = React.useRef<Owner | null>(null);\n+  const version = React.useRef(0);\n+  const recorded = React.useRef(recordedQuestionIds);\n+  recorded.current = recordedQuestionIds;\n+  const [pendingFlags, setPendingFlags] = React.useState(new Map<number, boolean>());\n+  const [statuses, setStatuses] = React.useState(new Map<number, RcAnnotationSaveStatus>());\n+  const [errors, setErrors] = React.useState(new Map<number, string>());\n+  const [recoveryError, setRecoveryError] = React.useState<string | null>(null);\n+  const matches = (owner: Owner | null) => !!owner && !owner.cancelled && enabled && owner.actor === actorId && owner.session === sessionId;\n+\n+  React.useEffect(() => {\n+    const owner: Owner = { actor: actorId ?? '', session: sessionId ?? '', cancelled: false, pending: new Map(), saving: new Map(), chains: new Map() };\n+    context.current = owner;\n+    const flags = new Map<number, boolean>();\n+    const recovered = new Map<number, RcAnnotationSaveStatus>();\n+    setErrors(new Map());\n+    setRecoveryError(null);\n+    if (actorId && sessionId && enabled) {\n+      try {\n+        const prefix = prefixFor(actorId, sessionId);\n+        for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {\n+          const key = localStorage.key(i);\n+          if (!key?.startsWith(prefix)) continue;\n+          const questionId = Number(key.slice(prefix.length));\n+          const value = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) ?? 'null') as { flagged: boolean } | null;\n+          if (!Number.isSafeInteger(questionId) || questionId < 1 || typeof value?.flagged !== 'boolean') throw new Error('Pending flags could not be read.');\n+          owner.pending.set(questionId, { flagged: value.flagged, version: ++version.current });\n+          flags.set(questionId, value.flagged);\n+          recovered.set(questionId, 'recovered');\n+        }\n+      } catch (error: unknown) {\n+        setRecoveryError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Pending flags could not be read.');\n+      }\n+    }\n+    setPendingFlags(flags);\n+    setStatuses(recovered);\n+    return () => { owner.cancelled = true; };\n+  }, [actorId, sessionId, enabled]);\n+\n+  function acknowledge(owner: Owner, questionId: number, intent: Intent) {\n+    const raw = localStorage.getItem(keyFor(owner, questionId));\n+    if (raw && JSON.parse(raw).flagged === intent.flagged && owner.pending.get(questionId)?.version === intent.version) localStorage.removeItem(keyFor(owner, questionId));\n+    if (matches(owner) && owner.pending.get(questionId)?.version === intent.version) {\n+      owner.pending.delete(questionId);\n+      setPendingFlags((map) => { const next = new Map(map); next.delete(questionId); return next; });\n+      setStatuses((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, 'saved'));\n+      setErrors((map) => { const next = new Map(map); next.delete(questionId); return next; });\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  function retry(questionId: number) {\n+    const owner = context.current;\n+    if (!matches(owner) || !owner) return;\n+    const intent = owner.pending.get(questionId);\n+    if (!intent || owner.saving.get(questionId) === intent.version) return;\n+    if (!recorded.current.includes(questionId)) return;\n+    owner.saving.set(questionId, intent.version);\n+    setStatuses((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, 'saving'));\n+    const previous = owner.chains.get(questionId) ?? Promise.resolve();\n+    const task = previous.catch(() => undefined).then(async () => {\n+      if (owner.cancelled) return;\n+      try {\n+        await setAttemptFlag(owner.session, questionId, intent.flagged, owner.actor);\n+        acknowledge(owner, questionId, intent);\n+      } catch (error: unknown) {\n+        if (matches(owner) && owner.pending.get(questionId)?.version === intent.version) {\n+          setStatuses((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, 'error'));\n+          setErrors((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'The flag was not saved.'));\n+        }\n+      } finally {\n+        if (owner.saving.get(questionId) === intent.version) owner.saving.delete(questionId);\n+      }\n+    });\n+    owner.chains.set(questionId, task);\n+  }\n+\n+  function setFlag(questionId: number, flagged: boolean) {\n+    const owner = context.current;\n+    if (!matches(owner) || !owner) return;\n+    const intent = { flagged, version: ++version.current };\n+    owner.pending.set(questionId, intent);\n+    setPendingFlags((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, flagged));\n+    try {\n+      localStorage.setItem(keyFor(owner, questionId), JSON.stringify({ flagged }));\n+      setStatuses((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, 'recovered'));\n+    } catch {\n+      setStatuses((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, 'error'));\n+      setErrors((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, 'The device could not keep a recovery copy. Retry saving before leaving.'));\n+    }\n+    retry(questionId);\n+  }\n+\n+  function acknowledgeRecordedFlag(questionId: number, flagged: boolean) {\n+    const owner = context.current;\n+    if (!matches(owner) || !owner) return;\n+    const intent = owner.pending.get(questionId);\n+    if (intent?.flagged === flagged) {\n+      try { acknowledge(owner, questionId, intent); }\n+      catch { setStatuses((map) => new Map(map).set(questionId, 'error')); }\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  const valid = matches(context.current);\n+  return { pendingFlags: valid ? pendingFlags : new Map<number, boolean>(), setFlag, retry, acknowledgeRecordedFlag,\n+    recoveryError: valid ? recoveryError : null,\n+    statusForQuestion: (id: number): RcAnnotationSaveStatus => valid ? statuses.get(id) ?? 'idle' : 'idle',\n+    errorForQuestion: (id: number) => valid ? errors.get(id) ?? null : null,\n+  };\n+}\n*** End Patch"));

Promise.resolve();\n+  const next = previous.catch(() => undefined).then(write);\n+  aidWriteChains.set(key, next);\n+  void next.finally(() => { if (aidWriteChains.get(key) === next) aidWriteChains.delete(key); }).catch(() => undefined);\n+  return next;\n+}\n+\n+export function setAttemptFlag(sessionId: string, questionId: number, flagged: boolean, expectedActorId?: string): Promise<void> {\n+  const queue = (actor: string) => serialAidWrite(`flag:${actor}:${sessionId}:${questionId}`, () => writeAttemptFlag(sessionId, questionId, flagged, actor));\n+  return expectedActorId ? queue(expectedActorId) : requireUserId().then(queue);\n+}\n+\n+async function writeAttemptFlag(\n@@\n-export async function saveAnnotationsNow(\n+export function saveAnnotationsNow(passageId: number, highlights: RcHighlight[], expectedActorId?: string): Promise<void> {\n+  const queue = (actor: string) => serialAidWrite(`annotation:${actor}:${passageId}`, () => writeAnnotations(passageId, highlights, actor));\n+  return expectedActorId ? queue(expectedActorId) : requireUserId().then(queue);\n+}\n+\n+async function writeAnnotations(\n*** End Patch"));

## assistant

Implemented tickets06–07 hook/API layer only.

Owned files:

- `useRcHighlights.ts` and tests.
- `rcProgress.ts` and tests.
- New `rcAnnotationRecovery.ts`.
- New `useRcFlagPersistence.ts` and tests.

Annotations now gate edits on successful actor-filtered hydration, retain pending intent under actor+passage, serialize writes across hook remounts, and expose acknowledged status/Retry. Flags use actor+session+question recovery, preserve source restrictions, and acknowledge initial-answer flags without losing newer intent.

Both recovery systems store unique intent tokens. Older acknowledgements cannot erase newer A/B/A recovery, even across remount and teardown. Adapter acknowledgements validate returned owner/content, with JSONB property order handled correctly.

**Verification:** 44/44 focused tests passed; targeted ESLint passed without warnings. Deferred tests cover account changes, hydration failure, rejected saves, older replies, cross-remount ordering and A/B/A recovery.

Earlier typecheck exposed only other workers’ files, already reported. No player edits, commits, pushes, deployments or source-answer changes performed. Live read-after-write acceptance remains for root integration verification.
